FiveM for GTA V Enhanced supports only the latest gamebuild (The Kortz Center Heist), plus `sv_enforceGameBuild 1` for the base game without DLC. ✅ [35][39]

### 8.8 Regional, censorship & content-change notes (GTA V-specific)

| Item | Platform | Region | Date | Notes | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Torture sequence cut | PS3/X360 (and later) | JP | 2013-10-10 → | The torture gameplay in the mission "By the Book" was removed from the Japanese release. | ✅ | [2] |
| Japanese PC version | PC | JP | 2015-04-14 | Built on the North American version; apart from text localisation, the changes applied to the Japanese console versions were not applied. The PC version did not receive a CERO rating. | ✅ | [15] |
| CERO rating (consoles) | X360 (2013); PS4 (2018 Premium Online Edition) | JP | 2013-10-10; 2018-06-14 | **CERO Z** (18+ only). 4Gamer's Xbox 360 product page gives "CERO Z：18歳以上のみ対象", release 2013-10-10, publishers Take-Two Interactive Japan / Rockstar Games, ¥7,770 incl. tax. Famitsu's 2018 announcement of the PS4 *Premium Online Edition* gives "CERO：Z", 2018-06-14, ¥7,400 excl. tax. The PS3, XB1, PS5 and XSX ratings are presumably also Z, but no fetched page states them individually. | ✅ (X360, PS4) / ⚠️ (other platforms) | [52][53] |
| Retail withdrawal | PS4/XB1 | AU | 2014-12 | Target and Kmart Australia pulled the game after a petition with >40,000 signatures. This was a retailer decision; the game was not reclassified. | ✅ | [2] |
| Transgender sex-worker models removed | PS5/XSX\|S (2022 re-release) | WW | 2022 (Wikipedia says April 2022, which conflicts with the 2022-03-15 launch) | Removed except in Director Mode. | ⚠️ | [2] |
| Gambling restricted | all current-gen | some regions (incl. some US states) | 2019-07-23 → | In-game casino gambling unavailable in certain regions (Diamond Casino & Resort). | ✅ | [7] |
| Saudi Arabia / UAE release | all | SA, AE | 2025-07-17 | Listed as a separate regional release date. Content changes, if any, **not documented**. | ⚠️ | [16] |

### 8.9 Cross-references

- **GTA Online** content updates, DLC descriptions and event weeks: **Section 9**. The TU numbers above map to them.
- **Compilations/bundles** that contain GTA V (e.g. *Grand Theft Auto V: Premium Edition* store bundles and multi-game packs): **Section 12**.
- **Leaks** (Sept 2022 Rockstar breach, Dec 2023 source-code leak and the cut GTA V DLC it exposed): **Section 11**. Leaked and preserved GTA V builds are listed in §8.1 and indexed in §11.7.

### 8.10 Coverage & known gaps (Section 8)

- **Console TU list:** complete as a list of numbers (1.00 → 1.73) from two community sources [3][4], cross-checked against Rockstar Support note titles [21][32][40][43]–[46]. Some TUs have one-day date conflicts (flagged). **Resolved (2nd pass):**
  - Community numbering skips 1.55/1.56, but Rockstar Support's own page numbers run 1.53→1.54→1.56→1.61 for 2021–22, offset from the community numbers. A Rockstar "1.55" page was not found.
  - The 1.73 date conflict is resolved (07-14 launch, 07-16 follow-up).
  - All post-2024 sub-drops (PC-only, PS5-only, Enhanced-only) were added from GTA Wiki.
- **PlayStation package versions (new, §8.5b):** full PS4 list 01.02 → 01.57 (US/EU) and 01.54 (JP), and PS5 list 01.001.000 → 01.011.000, from ORBISPatches/ProsperoPatches. PS4 dates are package creation dates, not release dates. Xbox package versions are **not documented**.
- **PC Legacy builds:** 69 distinct executable version numbers are documented, from 1.0.335.1 to 1.0.3889.0: the 67 in SHVDN's enum (Steam/NoSteam pairs counted once) plus 1.0.335.1 (GTA Wiki) and 1.0.1734.0 (SHVDN comment). GTA Wiki's "350.3" conflicts with this list. 1.0.331.1 is now attested by a 2015-04-20 crash report; 1.0.323.1 is still unverified. 19 builds have a sourced date or named update: 14 via FiveM's build→DLC table and 5 from the GTA Wiki 2015 pages. 1.0.2215.0 → TU 1.53 has weak support (a URL slug only). About 50 hotfix and intermediate builds still have **no documented release date**. SHVDN support dates are upper bounds only, and every TU mapping not tied to a source is now labelled "Inferred from ordering — not sourced ❓". The 2nd pass checked all 57 GTA Wiki per-update pages from 2015 on (none after 2015-07-21 gives a PC build), the Rockstar Support notes and news articles. SteamDB was not usable (bot challenge), and archive.org snapshots of the Script Hook V page were not reachable in bulk. Those snapshots remain the most promising route to date individual builds (as upper bounds).
- **PC Enhanced builds:** 11 known (811.8 → 1158.13); release dates are mostly not documented.
- **Serials:** PS3 (BLUS/BLES/BLAS/BLJM + PSN NPUB/NPEB), Xbox 360 (TT-2215), PS4 (CUSA00411/00419/00880/01436) and PS5 (PPSA01721/03420/03421/04263/04264/04265) documented. Microsoft Store product IDs (BPJ686W6S0NH, 9NXMBTB02ZSF, 9P8RQH67TTT1, etc.) and Steam Enhanced app 3240220 were added in the 2nd pass. **Physical Xbox One / Series disc product codes not documented.**
- **Resolved in 2nd pass:** Epic giveaway (2020-05-14 → 05-21); PS Plus / Game Pass stints; CERO Z (X360 and PS4 confirmed).
- **Audit integration (§14 items):** added the GTA V pre-release and prototype builds from Hidden Palace (§8.1): the Oct 13, 2014 dev build QA v108 / 0.0.0.77, the 1.0.2699.0 source build, eight Xbox 360 PartnerNet DLC prototypes, PS3 patch prototypes 377 and 388.5, GTA5 Debug.exe and the PS3 dev-kit leftovers. Also added BLKS-20442 and NPJB00516 (⚠️, single list) and the iFruit and Manual companion apps (§8.4b). The old statement that no playable pre-release GTA V build had leaked was **wrong** and has been removed: the Oct 13, 2014 build leaked on 2026-09-18.
- **Still not documented:** confirmation of BLKS-20442 and NPJB00516 from a second source; iOS, Windows Phone, Windows and Vita version histories of the companion apps; a release date for each companion-app platform; the CERO rating stated individually for PS3/XB1/PS5/XSX; release dates for the PC Enhanced builds; the start date of the PS Now stint; Xbox package versions.
- Research note: the shared web-search quota and the hourly fetch limit ran out during this section. Rows marked "not documented" may be fillable in a follow-up pass, e.g. from the GTA Wiki per-TU pages after 2023, Rockstar Support TU notes 1.66–1.73, and SteamDB depot history.

## 9. GTA Online — Every Update & DLC

**Scope.** Every titled GTA Online content update / DLC from launch (1 Oct 2013) to the present (research date 24 Oct 2026), plus the cancelled GTA V single-player DLC, GTA+, Shark Cards, the Criminal Enterprise Starter Pack and the standalone GTA Online release. Weekly bonus events are left out unless they were a titled update with new content.

**Columns.**
- **Game version / TU** gives the console title-update ("patch") number. Until 2015 GTA V and GTA Online used separate numbering on each platform family: e.g. Heists is TU 1.21 on PS3/360 but 1.07 on PS4/XB1 (GTA Wiki). GTABase's master list uses one running number, so both are shown where they differ.
- **PC build** numbers (1.0.xxxx) come from the FiveM `sv_enforceGameBuild` table and the DurtyFree data-dump commit log, which record the PC build that shipped each DLC.
- **Internal DLC name** is the `dlcpacks:/` folder name: from dlclist.xml (a 2019 copy on Pastebin) for the older packs, and from the `DlcName` field of the DurtyFree vehicles.json dump plus the FiveM build aliases for the newer ones.
- **Conf.**: ✅ confirmed by ≥2 independent sources or an official Rockstar source; ⚠️ only one community source; ❓ rumour or conflicting. "not documented" means no source was found.

**Platform eras.** These cover what the Online servers existed on. Which versions a given update reached is in the tables.
- **PS3/X360**, from 1 Oct 2013. Servers closed 16 Dec 2021 [S1].
- **PS4/XB1**, from 18 Nov 2014 [S1].
- **PC**, from 14 Apr 2015 [S1]. The original PC version was renamed "GTA V Legacy" on 4 Mar 2025, when the free "GTA V Enhanced" upgrade launched [S1][S9].
- **PS5/XSX/S**, from 15 Mar 2022, with standalone GTA Online on the same day [S1][S10].

PC builds 2015–2016 seen as patch uploads on playground.ru (Russian community file site; dates are **upload** dates, and the uploads name no update) [S96] ⚠️: 1.0.393.2 ("Update 7", 10 Jul 2015), 1.0.393.4 ("Update 8", 9 Aug 2015), 1.0.463.1 (16 Sep 2015), 1.0.573.1 (17 Dec 2015), 1.0.678.1 (28 Apr 2016), 1.0.757.2 (12 Jun 2016), 1.0.757.4 (14 Jun 2016). Upload dates fall one or two days after Freemode Events (15 Sep 2015), Executives and Other Criminals (15 Dec 2015) and Finance and Felony (7 Jun 2016), but no source ties these builds to those updates. Any such pairing is inferred from dates only ❓.

The Rockstar Newswire GTA Online tag lists only weekly events, GTA+ posts and the Halloween event (1 Oct 2026) after the 14 Jul 2026 launch post, up to its latest post on 8 Oct 2026 [S90]. The next "winter 2026" update was not documented in any source consulted.

### 9.A GTA V single-player (Story Mode) DLC: none released

No story-mode expansion for GTA V was ever released. Every DLC listed above is GTA Online content. Some packs also added cars, weapons and clothing to story mode.

| Item | What is documented | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|
| Promised story updates | A Rockstar Newswire post of 9 Dec 2013 teased Story Mode additions for 2014. In Nov 2014 a Rockstar representative (Videogames Gameblog, via CVG, 12 Nov 2014) said the expansion would be "at least on the level of" *The Lost and Damned* / *The Ballad of Gay Tony*, with 12–20 hours of missions | ⚠️ | S15 |
| Cancellation | Imran Sarwar, then Rockstar's director of design, confirmed in a Game Informer interview on 23 Oct 2017 that single-player DLC was dropped to focus on GTA Online and Red Dead Redemption 2. Wikipedia says plans "were later scrapped" to focus on GTA Online and RDR2 | ✅ | S8, S15 |
| **"Agent Trevor"** (cancelled espionage episode starring Trevor) | Script references (`dlc_agenttrevor`, a mission list with codenames) were found in the TU 1.13/1.14 files and updated by the Dec 2023 source-code leak. The leak also used the name "Clifford", which GTA Wiki says was later debunked. Steven Ogg said (Apr 2024 podcast) that he recorded some work before it "just disappeared". Joe Rubino said about half was finished. Dan Houser said in an Oct 2025 interview that it was half complete and was abandoned for RDR2. GTA Wiki says parts of the content were reused in Online (e.g. the Stromberg in The Doomsday Heist, Valkyrie and Insurgent in Heists) | ⚠️ (one wiki citing several interviews; parts marked unverified) | S15 |
| Other cancelled packs | After the 24 Dec 2023 source-code leak, "dataminers found at least eight sets of single-player DLC were in development but cancelled, including a Liberty City expansion" (Wikipedia). GTA Wiki lists a Zombie/"Norman" episode, an Alien Invasion episode, "LibertyV" (a Liberty City map), a North Yankton "Prologue" expansion, and Relationships and Manhunt packs | ⚠️ / ❓ (leak-derived) | S8, S15 |

### 9.D Criminal Enterprise Starter Pack and standalone GTA Online

| Item | Detail | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Criminal Enterprise Starter Pack** | Released 14 Dec 2017 on PS4, XB1 and PC without prior announcement, at US$39.99. Contents: GTA$1,000,000 plus properties (Executive Office, Bunker, Counterfeit Cash Factory, Biker Clubhouse, 2-car apartment, 10-car garage), 10 vehicles, weapons, 31 outfits and 61 tattoos, about GTA$12.05M in asset value. Bundled with retail GTA V as the **Premium Online Edition** from 20 Apr 2018. By 21 Dec 2017 three further store bundles pairing the pack with a Great White, Whale or Megalodon Shark Card were on the Microsoft, Sony and Steam stores (GTAboom, 21 Dec 2017) [S95] ⚠️. Discontinuation or replacement: **not documented**. On PS5/XSX the **Career Builder** fills the starter role (pick one of four businesses and get GTA$4M) | ⚠️ | S10, S13 |
| **Standalone GTA Online** | Released 15 Mar 2022 for PS5 and XSX/S alongside Expanded & Enhanced GTA V. Free on PS5 for the first three months (until 14 Jun 2022). On Xbox Series X it cost US$9.99 / £8.99 for the first three months (GINX). Standard price after 14 Jun 2022: **US$19.99 / £17.99** on both PS5 (Push Square, 7–8 Mar 2022) and Xbox Series X (GINX, 10 Mar 2022). For comparison, the PS5 story-mode-only GTA V was US$9.99 until 14 Jun 2022 and the combined package US$39.99 afterwards (Push Square) | ✅ | S1, S11, S93, S94 |
| Progress transfer | One-time PS4→PS5 / XB1→XSX transfer. From San Andreas Mercenaries (13 Jun 2023) the profile is copied rather than moved. There is no cross-family transfer | ⚠️ | S1, S10 |

### 9.F Gaps and conflicts

**Conflicting values** (both are shown in the tables):
- Halloween Surprise TU: 1.30 era (GTABase; GTA Wiki TU table) vs "1.69" (GTA Wiki infobox).
- Safehouse PC build: 3717 (DurtyFree, launch) vs 3751 (FiveM table).

**Resolved in the second pass** (primary Rockstar sources): Beach Bum = 19 Nov 2013, Deathmatch & Race Creators = 11 Dec 2013, High Life = 13 May 2014, Lowriders = 20 Oct 2015, A Safehouse in the Hills = 10 Dec 2025, Kortz Center Heist = 14 Jul 2026 (Rockstar Newswire "Now Available" posts). Criminal Enterprises = TU 1.61 and Drug Wars = TU 1.64 (Rockstar Support TU notes). The other dates in each pair are wiki infobox, year-page or notes-block dates.

**Not documented:**
- PC builds for Online 1.21–1.35 except the playground.ru uploads listed under 2016 (none tied to an update), and builds 1.0.944–1.0.1365 (Cunning Stunts to SSASSS).
- The PC build for the Diamond Casino Heist (TU 1.49/1.50). TU 1.48 = 1.0.1737.0 rests on one community source ⚠️.
- The GTA V Enhanced (PC) build at launch.
- Internal packs for the Content Creator, Capture and Capture Creator updates. The `file_name` field is blank on both gta.wiki and gta.fandom.com, and the 2019 dlclist names no pack for them.
- The exact first-sale date of Shark Cards. Both GTA Wikis say only that GTA Online "launched with" the Red, Tiger, Bull and Great White cards.
- The Criminal Enterprise Starter Pack's end of sale.
- Whether PS4/XB1 GTA Online support has ended. As of the TU 1.73 notes (2026), PS4/XB1 are still listed.

**Research limits:**
- gta.fandom.com was readable in the second pass through the browser (MediaWiki API). Its infoboxes match gta.wiki, which is a copy, so the two are not counted as independent sources.
- gtaforums.com (the Script Hook V topic, which logs PC build numbers) was behind a Cloudflare challenge. web.archive.org needed approval for every page load, so old Rockstar Support (Zendesk) note URLs could not be read; they now return 404.

## 10. Grand Theft Auto: The Trilogy – The Definitive Edition

_Remastered compilation of Grand Theft Auto III, Vice City and San Andreas. Developed by Grove Street Games (formerly War Drum Studios) on Unreal Engine 4, published by Rockstar Games. Each of the three games is a separately installed application with its own title ID and its own update stream on every platform; the "Trilogy" is a store bundle of the three. Mobile versions (Netflix Games, 2023) are attributed by Wikipedia (citing Eurogamer.pl, 2024-01-24) to Video Games Deluxe ⚠️ (single secondary source; Netflix's announcement credits only Rockstar Games) — see 10.5._

Confidence key: ✅ confirmed by primary source or ≥2 independent sources · ⚠️ single community/secondary source · ❓ rumour/unverified.

### 10.1 Pre-release, announcement and launch timeline

| Date | Event | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2021-08-12 | Kotaku reports remastered trilogy in development (initially attributed to Rockstar Dundee) | ✅ (report) | 1 |
| 2021-09-30 (reported) | South Korea's Game Rating and Administration Committee rates "Grand Theft Auto: The Trilogy – The Definitive Edition" (spotted by Nibel) | ✅ | 1, 4 |
| 2021-10-06 (reported) | Rockstar Games Launcher update found to contain logos, artwork and achievements for the trilogy (datamined) | ✅ | 1 |
| 2021-10-08 | Official announcement by Rockstar (20th-anniversary month of GTA III): PS5, Xbox Series, PS4, Xbox One, Switch, PC (Rockstar Games Launcher) in 2021; iOS/Android announced for first half of 2022 (actually released 2023-12-14) | ✅ | 1, 3 |
| 2021-10-13 | Original (pre-DE) versions of GTA III, Vice City, San Andreas delisted from digital console and PC stores (GTA Wiki gives 2021-10-11 — dates conflict) | ✅ (event) / ⚠️ (exact day) | 1, 46 |
| 2021-10-22 | Pre-orders open; first full trailer and screenshots | ✅ | 1 |
| 2021-11-11 | Digital release on PS4, PS5, Xbox One, Xbox Series X\|S, Nintendo Switch, Windows (Rockstar Games Launcher). PlayStation Store error unlocked it early in Australia/New Zealand | ✅ | 1 |
| 2021-11-11 → 2021-11-14 | Rockstar Games Launcher offline ~28 h; Windows version unplayable/withdrawn until 14 Nov. GTA Wiki attributes the PC pull (12–15 Nov) to files found for the "Hot Coffee" minigame and ~20 songs previously removed for licensing reasons | ✅ (outage) / ⚠️ (reason) | 1, 46 |
| 2021-11-19 | Rockstar statement/apology; announces originals will return as a bundle on the Rockstar Store | ✅ | 1 |
| 2021-11-30 | Rockstar announces physical delay: PS4/Xbox One/Xbox Series X moved 7 Dec → 17 Dec 2021; Switch physical moved to "early 2022" (later 11 Feb 2022) | ✅ | 6, 1 |
| 2021-12-03 | Original PC versions delivered free to DE owners on Rockstar Launcher (offer for purchases until 2022-06-30) | ✅ | 1 |
| 2022-12-06 (reported) | Rockstar Games Launcher update found containing Epic IDs tagged `gta3unreal`, `gtavcunreal`, `gtasaunreal` and Steam IDs for the three games (datamined) | ⚠️ (single outlet) | 15 |
| 2023-01-19 | Steam release (3 separate apps, Rockstar Launcher still required; Steam Deck "Playable") | ✅ | 16, 17, 18, 1 |
| 2023-02-15 | Epic Games Store release (year inferred by Wikipedia from context) | ⚠️ | 1 |
| 2023-11-29 | Netflix announces mobile versions; pre-registration opens | ✅ | 19 |
| 2023-12-14 | iOS/Android release via Netflix Games (App Store, Google Play, Netflix app) | ✅ | 19, 1 |
| 2024-11-12/13 | Title Update 1.112 (Classic Lighting etc.); Grove Street Games removed from intro/main menu | ✅ | 2, 20, 1 |
| 2024-12-13 | Last day for GTA III and Vice City DE on Netflix (licence expiry) | ✅ | 21, 1 |
| 2025-12 | San Andreas DE removed from Netflix | ⚠️ (Wikipedia citing mobile sales tracker) | 1 |

**Engine / basis.** Unreal Engine 4 replaced the originals' RenderWare engine; physics code carried over from the originals; >100,000 textures AI-upscaled then hand-adjusted (Wikipedia citing USA Today/TheGamer) ✅ [1]. The launch press release credits the games as "originally developed by Rockstar Games and adapted to modern platforms by Grove Street Games", lists "Unreal Engine", 4K/up to 60 FPS on PS5 and Xbox Series X, NVIDIA DLSS on PC, gyro aiming and touchscreen camera on Switch ✅ [5]. SteamDB lists technologies "Unreal Engine, Rockstar Launcher" ✅ [17].

**Datamined/removed content.** Per Wikipedia, journalists reported (based on data miners) that removed content included radio songs, developer notes and the "Hot Coffee" minigame files ⚠️ [1]. SteamDB shows all three Steam app IDs were first created on 2021-02-05 (changelists #10666223/#10666229/#10666235), ~8 months before announcement ✅ [16, 17, 43].

**Removal of originals and return.** Rockstar's 2021-10-08 announcement stated the existing versions "will be removed from digital storefronts next week" ✅ [3]; delisted 2021-10-13 [1]. On 2021-11-19 Rockstar said the original PC versions would return on the Rockstar Store as a bundle and be free to DE PC buyers (purchases through 2022-06-30) ✅ [7, 1]; delivered 2021-12-03 [1].


### 10.2 Platforms, editions and title IDs

Each game is a separate application; stores also sell the three-game "Trilogy" bundle. Physical PS4/Xbox discs were scheduled for 2021-12-07 and slipped to 2021-12-17; Switch physical slipped to 2022-02-11. No PS5 or PC physical release appears in Rockstar's physical-date announcements (not documented).

### 10.3a Per-platform package / build versions (PS4, PS5, Switch)

- **PS4:** ORBISPatches **creation dates**, i.e. when the package was built, usually days before release.
- **PS5:** ProsperoPatches **"Date Added"**, i.e. when the tracker first saw the package.
- **Switch:** blawar/titledb `versions.json` dates, i.e. when each update version was first registered. titledb stores update versions as integers (multiples of 65536). Nintendo Life's reported TU 1.02 Switch versions match them: GTA III 1.0.3 = v196608 (3×65536) and VC 1.0.4 = v262144 (4×65536), both dated 2021-11-22. The "1.0.n = n×65536" reading of the other versions is **inferred from that pattern — not sourced** ❓, so the raw integers are given.
- **Matching TU:** given only where a package date coincides with a Rockstar TU date from §10.3; otherwise "—".

**PS5** (all nine PPSA IDs: III 03527/03528/03529, VC 03530/03531/03532, SA 03524/03525/03526) carry identical version numbers and dates [40]:

| PS5 pkg | First seen | PKG size (III / VC / SA, US) | Matching TU |
|---|---|---|---|
| 01.000.000 | 2021-11-03 | 4.9 / 10 / 21.1 GB | Pre-launch package (launch 2021-11-11) |
| 01.001.000 | 2021-11-10 | 5 / 10.5 / 21.4 GB | Day-one package |
| 01.002.000 | 2021-11-19 | 4.8 / 10.3 / 21.3 GB | TU 1.02 (2021-11-19) |
| 01.003.000 | 2021-11-30 | 4.9 / 10.4 / 21.6 GB | TU 1.03 (2021-11-30) |
| 01.005.000 | 2022-02-28 | 5.1 / 11.2 / 23.3 GB | TU 1.04 (2022-02-28) |
| 01.006.000 | 2022-10-18 | 5.1 / 11.1 / 23.3 GB | TU 1.04.5 (2022-10-18) |
| **01.007.000** | 2024-11-12 | 5.1 / 11.1 / 23.3 GB | TU 1.112 (2024-11-12) — latest |

**Conflict:** ProsperoPatches lists **no 01.004.000** for any of the nine PS5 IDs, but MP1st reported a San Andreas PS5 "1.004" on 2021-12-03 [13]. ⚠️

**PS4** (all nine CUSA IDs; same version numbers, creation dates within a day or two of each other) [39]:

| PS4 pkg | Creation date(s) | PKG size (III / VC / SA) | ORBISPatches description |
|---|---|---|---|
| 01.01 | 2021-11-02 | 0.55–0.56 GB / 5.9 GB / 2.1–2.2 GB | "Fixes and Performance improvements" (day-one) |
| 01.02 | 2021-11-16 | 4.7 / 10.2 / 21.2 GB | "Update 01.02 - Includes a range of across-the-board improvements…" |
| 01.03 | 2021-11-24 | 4.7 / 10.2 / 21.2 GB | — (no description) |
| 01.04 | 2021-11-30 / 2021-12-01 | 4.7 / 10.2 / 21.2 GB | — (no description) |
| 01.05 | III 2022-01-19; VC 2022-01-28/29; SA 2022-02-08/09 | 4.7 / 10.2 / 21.2 GB | "Includes a range of across-the-board improvements and bug fixes" |
| 01.06 | 2022-09-01 / 2022-09-02 | 4.7 / 10.1 / 21.1 GB | "Stability and performance improvements" |
| **01.07** | 2024-10-02 | 4.7 / 10.1 / 21.1 GB | "Various bugfixes and improvements" — latest |

Which public TU each PS4 package shipped as is not stated by ORBISPatches. The creation dates precede the TU dates in §10.3 (1.02 → 2021-11-19; 1.03 → 11-30; Dec-3 stability update; 1.04 → 2022-02-28; 1.04.5 → 2022-10-18; 1.112 → 2024-11-13), but matching them by order is **inferred — not sourced** ❓.

**Nintendo Switch** update versions (titledb) [56]:

| Game (title ID) | Versions → date first registered |
|---|---|
| GTA III – DE (0100C3C012718000) | v65536 2021-10-28 · v131072 2021-11-03 · v196608 2021-11-22 · v327680 2021-12-02 · v393216 2022-02-24 · v458752 2022-10-18 · **v524288 2024-11-16** |
| Vice City – DE (0100182014022000) | v131072 2021-10-28 · v196608 2021-11-03 · v262144 2021-11-22 · v327680 2021-12-02 · v393216 2022-02-24 · v458752 2022-10-18 · **v524288 2024-11-16** |
| San Andreas – DE (010065A014024000) | v65536 2021-10-28 · v131072 2021-11-03 · v196608 2021-11-22 · v327680 2021-12-02 · v393216 2022-02-24 · v458752 2022-10-18 · **v524288 2024-11-16** |

Points to note:
- The Switch TU 1.03 build, which Rockstar said was coming "in the coming days" after 2021-11-30, corresponds to the update registered **2021-12-02** for all three games (v327680).
- GTA III and SA skip v262144 there.
- The TU 1.04 Switch build (v393216) was registered 2022-02-24, matching reports that the Switch notes surfaced before the 2022-02-28 rollout.
- tinfoil.io shows GTA III – DE at "Version v8" [57]. ✅

**December 2024 update?** No December 2024 console/PC update was found; the restored-lighting/credit-change update is November 2024 (TU 1.112). December 2024 is when GTA III and Vice City left Netflix [1, 21].

**Developer reaction.** Grove Street Games CEO Thomas Williamson: "Speaking entire hypothetically: It's a dick move to remove primary developers from credits in an update, especially when an update includes hundreds of fixes that were provided by those developers … that stayed out of players' hands for years" ✅ [32, 1].

### 10.5 Mobile versions (iOS / Android)

Released via Netflix Games on 2023-12-14 (pre-registration from 2023-11-29), listed on the App Store, Google Play and inside the Netflix app, with no ads/IAP for members [19]. Wikipedia attributes the ports to Video Games Deluxe ⚠️ [1] and quotes the Nov 2023 announcement as "available for Netflix subscribers and individual purchase" ⚠️ [1]. Download figures (Wikipedia): >18 M by Jan 2024; >30 M by Jun 2024; SA reached 57.4 M by Nov 2025 ⚠️ [1]. GTA III and VC were removed from Netflix after 2024-12-13 ("games will be removed from Netflix as licenses expire") ✅ [21]; San Andreas removed Dec 2025 ⚠️ [1]. Netflix-era Android version numbers are now documented from APKMirror's upload history [58]; dates are APKMirror upload dates. iOS Netflix-era version numbers are still not documented.

Rockstar Support nevertheless lists 2025 mobile title updates for all three DE games (after III/VC left Netflix); the pages do not say through which storefront these builds ship ⚠️.

| Version | Platform | Region | Release date | Serial / ID | Changes | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Netflix launch build | iOS, Android | WW | 2023-12-14 | Android **1.72.42919648** for all three games (APKMirror uploads dated 2023-12-14; target API 33, min. Android 11). iOS version not documented. | Launch of III, VC, SA – DE | ✅ | 19, 1, 58 |
| Netflix update | Android | WW | 2024-04-08 (upload) | GTA III & VC **1.83.44255649**; SA **1.86.44544238** | — | ✅ (as uploaded) | 58 |
| Netflix update | Android | WW | 2024-11-12 (upload) | GTA III & VC **1.90.48899882** (no matching SA upload on APKMirror) | Same day as console/PC TU 1.112 | ✅ (as uploaded) | 58 |
| GTA III – DE v1.84.0 | iOS | WW | 2025-07-09 | — | "General fixes and improvements" | ✅ | 34 |
| GTA III – DE v1.84.15 | Android | WW | 2025-07-09 | — | General fixes and improvements | ✅ | 34 |
| GTA III – DE v1.84.1 | iOS | WW | 2025-07-17 | — | General fixes and improvements | ✅ | 34 |
| GTA III – DE v1.84.18 | Android | WW | 2025-07-17 | — | General fixes and improvements | ✅ | 34 |
| GTA III – DE v1.84.2 | iOS | WW | 2025-08-18 | — | General fixes and improvements | ✅ | 34 |
| GTA III – DE v1.84.21 | Android | WW | 2025-08-18 | — | General fixes and improvements | ✅ | 34 |

**Standalone (non-Netflix) Rockstar Google Play apps.** Google Play also has three separate Rockstar Games listings with the package suffix **`.de`**. The store names show it stands for "**Definitive**", not German: "GTA III - Definitive", "GTA: Vice City - Definitive" and "GTA: San Andreas - Definitive". The III and VC descriptions offer a **30-minute free trial**, then a one-time in-app purchase of the full game ($19.99 per item, US store), or unlimited access for **GTA+** members while the game is in GTA+. Read on the US store, 2026-10-24 [62]:

| App (package) | Version (Play "About this game") | Play "Released on" | Play "Updated on" | Requires | Downloads | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GTA III - Definitive (`com.rockstargames.gta3.de`) | **1.84.3** | 2023-12-08 | 2025-11-05 | Android 11+ | 1,000,000+ | ✅ (primary store page) | 62 |
| GTA: Vice City - Definitive (`com.rockstargames.gtavc.de`) | **1.90.0** | 2023-12-08 | 2025-09-04 | Android 11+ | 5,000,000+ | ✅ (primary store page) | 62 |
| GTA: San Andreas - Definitive (`com.rockstargames.gtasa.de`) | **1.87.0** | 2023-12-08 | 2025-11-04 | Android 11+ | 50,000+ | ✅ (primary store page) | 62 |

Notes on this table:
- The "Released on" date of **2023-12-08** is Google Play's own field. It is six days earlier than the 2023-12-14 Netflix launch and may be the date the listing was created; Play does not say when the paid/trial model began.
- The Play versions of III (1.84.3) and SA (1.87.0) are the same numbers as Rockstar Support's 2025 Android title updates in the table above. That suggests, but does not state, that those 2025 builds ship through these standalone apps. Rockstar's Play "Updated on" dates (2025-11-05 / 2025-11-04) are one week **earlier** than Rockstar Support's 2025-11-12 entries, so both dates are kept ⚠️.
- For Vice City, Play shows **1.90.0** (updated 2025-09-04), while the latest Rockstar Support VC Android entry is v1.84.5 (2025-08-28). This is a numbering conflict ⚠️.
- The brief's "(German)" label for these packages is **wrong**: the store names show `.de` means Definitive.
- No other San Andreas DE standalone package was found; the guessed variants returned 404, and `com.rockstargames.gtasa.de` is the SA equivalent.

Note: the separate classic **San Andreas mobile** port (War Drum Studios, 2013; Android v1.03 → v2.11.311, iOS v1.01 → v2.2.21) is a different product with its own Rockstar Support page and is out of scope here [37]. Rockstar Support (updated 2026-08-18) states Rockstar titles on iOS require iOS 17+ for further updates/support (titles not enumerated) ⚠️ [53].

### 10.6 Gaps / not documented

- **Resolved in 2nd pass (2026-10-24):**
  - Full PS4, PS5 and Switch package/version histories (§10.3a).
  - Switch title IDs and NSUIDs; Switch NA cartridge code HAC-P-A5RBA (⚠️ single catalogue).
  - Microsoft Store product IDs.
  - Android package names and Netflix-era Android versions; SA iOS App Store ID.
  - Confirmation that no console/PC update after 1.112 exists in the trackers.
- Still not documented:
  - Xbox per-build version numbers; Steam/Epic build history beyond the Deck-test builds (SteamDB not usable: bot challenge).
  - Whether the Switch cartridge holds all three games; EU Switch cartridge code; PS4 disc serials (CUSA disc variants); Xbox disc product codes.
  - Asian-region PS4/PS5 title IDs.
  - iOS App Store IDs for III and VC; iOS Netflix-era version numbers. Storefront for the 2025 Android builds: *partly resolved*, since the standalone Rockstar Google Play apps `com.rockstargames.gta3.de` / `gtavc.de` / `gtasa.de` carry matching version numbers (§10.5), but the iOS standalone equivalents are not documented.
  - Japanese disc/cartridge codes. (Resolved: JP digital release 2021-11-11 with CERO Z; JP PS4/Switch package edition 2022-01-27.)
- **Open conflict:** PS5 "1.004" (MP1st, 2021-12-03) vs ProsperoPatches (no 01.004.000).

## 11. Grand Theft Auto VI, Leaks, Cancelled & Lost Projects

> Scope: Grand Theft Auto VI (status as researched on 2026-10-24, i.e. **before** its scheduled 2026-11-19 launch), the major leaks affecting the series (2022 Rockstar hack, 2023 trailer leak, 2023 GTA V source-code leak, 2026 "CyberLeek" leaks), an index of known leaked prototype/debug builds across all games, and cancelled / unreleased / lost projects. Legend: ✅ confirmed by official source or multiple reputable outlets; ⚠️ single community source, secondhand report, or fan-site only; ❓ rumour / unverified claim. "Not documented" = searched for but no evidence found.

### 11.1 Grand Theft Auto VI — development & announcement timeline

| Date | Event | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2014 | Preliminary work on the next GTA reportedly began after GTA V's release (Bloomberg reporting, as summarised by Wikipedia). | ⚠️ | [1] |
| Late 2018 | Early development reportedly began after Red Dead Redemption 2 shipped. | ⚠️ | [1] |
| 2020 | Principal production began; internal codename reported as **"Project Americas"** (leaked 2022 file names also contained "Americas"). | ✅ | [1][9] |
| 2022-02-04 | Rockstar confirms, in a Newswire post about GTA V next-gen/GTA Online and on Twitter: "active development for the next entry in the Grand Theft Auto series is well underway." First official acknowledgement. | ✅ | [2] |
| 2022-09-18 | Rockstar network intrusion: ~90 development videos of GTA VI posted on GTAForums by "teapotuberhacker" (see 11.3). | ✅ | [7][8][9] |
| 2022-09-19 | Rockstar statement confirms the "network intrusion" and that development footage was stolen; says development will continue as planned. | ✅ | [8] |
| 2023-11-08 | Rockstar (statement by Sam Houser) announces the first trailer of the next GTA for early December 2023, Rockstar's 25th anniversary. | ✅ | [1][35] |
| 2024-05-16 | Take-Two FY2024 results: GTA VI targeted for **Fall (calendar) 2025**. | ✅ | [36] |
| 2023-12-04 (evening ET) | **Trailer 1** leaked online (low-quality copy); Rockstar released it early on YouTube ("Our trailer has leaked… watch the real thing"). Planned premiere had been 2023-12-05 09:00 ET. Revealed title *Grand Theft Auto VI*, setting (Leonida / Vice City), release window **2025**, platforms **PS5 and Xbox Series X\|S**. Rockstar's own site dates Trailer 1 as **4 December 2023**; Newswire post is dated 5 December 2023 in some listings. | ✅ | [3][4][5] |
| 2024-02 | Rockstar ordered staff back to offices five days a week from April 2024, citing productivity and security (Bloomberg/IGN). | ✅ | [1] |
| 2025-05-02 | **Delay #1**: Rockstar announces release date **26 May 2026** ("We are very sorry that this is later than you expected"). | ✅ | [10][11] |
| 2025-05-06 | **Trailer 2** released (recorded on PS5), plus website update with ~70 screenshots and character/location profiles. (Note: Trailer 2 is **2025-05-06**, not 2026.) | ✅ | [4][12] |
| 2025-10 | Rockstar dismisses 34 staff (31 Rockstar North, 3 Rockstar Toronto) for allegedly leaking confidential information; IWGB union alleges union-busting. | ✅ | [1][13] |
| 2025-11-06 | **Delay #2**: Rockstar announces new release date **19 November 2026**, citing extra polish. | ✅ | [14][15] |
| 2026-06-18 | Official cover art and new logo revealed; pre-order date (25 June) announced. | ✅ | [16][17] |
| 2026-06-24 | Pricing revealed: Standard **US$79.99**, Ultimate Edition **US$99.99**; pre-load from 2026-11-12. | ✅ | [18] |
| 2026-06-25 | Pre-orders open (PS5 / Xbox Series X\|S, digital storefronts and retail). | ✅ | [17][18] |
| 2026-08-06 | Rockstar announces *Grand Theft Auto VI: An Extended Look* to premiere on Netflix. | ✅ | [17][19] |
| 2026-08-18 → 08-26 | "CyberLeek" leaks of gameplay clips and alleged map (see 11.5). | ✅ | [20][21] |
| 2026-08-26 | Rockstar statement on leaks: "heartbreaking for our team"; release date reaffirmed. | ✅ | [21] |
| 2026-08-27 | *Grand Theft Auto VI: An Extended Look* premieres on Netflix (15:00 ET), then YouTube / rockstargames.com (21:00 ET); captured on PS5; Netflix suffered an outage at launch. | ✅ | [17][19][22] |
| 2026-08-28 | 30 fps on PS5 / PS5 Pro / Xbox Series reported — secondhand, via a YouTuber who visited Rockstar North (attributed to Rockstar's Rob Nelson). No direct Rockstar statement found. | ⚠️ | [23] |
| 2026-09-17 | *Grand Theft Auto VI: The Album* (with Atlantic Records) announced for 2026-11-19. | ✅ | [17][24] |
| 2026-09-24 | "The Goodtime State – Vice City Collection" collectible set pre-orders announced. | ⚠️ | [17] |
| ~2026-10-02 | ESRB **M (Mature 17+)** rating appears on store pages (descriptors reported: Blood and Gore, Intense Violence, Mature Humor, Nudity, Strong Language, Strong Sexual Content, Use of Drugs and Alcohol; In-Game Purchases). PEGI 18 (provisional on some storefronts), Australia R18+ (classification record 2026-08-27), NZ R18 (2026-10-02) per fan-site compilation. | ⚠️ | [25][26] |
| 2026-10-08 | Newswire: in-game radio stations feature; GTA VI merchandise at Rockstar Store. | ⚠️ | [17] |
| 2026-10-15 | **Launch Trailer** released. | ✅ | [27][28] |
| 2026-11-12 | Scheduled: pre-load opens; physical (code-in-box) copies at retail. | ✅ | [18] |
| **2026-11-19** | **Scheduled worldwide release, PS5 & Xbox Series X\|S.** As of 2026-10-24 no further delay announced. | ✅ | [14][18][27] |

**Release-date history (summary):** 2025 window (Trailer 1, Dec 2023) → "Fall 2025" (Take-Two guidance, May 2024 earnings, reiterated Feb 2025) → 26 May 2026 (announced 2025-05-02) → 19 November 2026 (announced 2025-11-06). ✅ [1][10][14]

### 11.2 Grand Theft Auto VI — editions, platforms, versions (as announced, pre-release)

| Item | Detail | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|
| Platforms at launch | PlayStation 5, Xbox Series X\|S. "Plays best on PS5" marketing. No PC version announced as of Oct 2026. | ✅ | [1][18][27] |
| Standard Edition | US$79.99 | ✅ | [18] |
| Ultimate Edition | US$99.99; digital extras (vehicles, weapons, clothing, hairstyles, tattoos, extra missions/locations). Sensor Tower estimated ~89% of pre-orders were Ultimate (via Wikipedia). | ✅ / ⚠️ (share) | [1][18] |
| Pre-order / early-purchase bonus | "Vintage Vice City Pack" ('55 Vapid Stanier + garage, 80s outfits/hairstyles, pastel weapon skin) for purchases before 2026-11-20. | ✅ | [18] |
| Physical edition | Box containing a download code — no disc. Pre-load from 2026-11-12. | ✅ | [1][18] |
| **Ultimate Edition Upgrade** (separate SKU) | Rockstar Support: the Ultimate Edition is the game plus the "GTAVI: Ultimate Edition Upgrade" ('95 Grotti Cheetah, Hawk & Little Morgan revolvers, personalised weapon variants, Vice City Styles, Jason's safehouse vehicles, Ganado Retro Build, Rideout Customs mod shop, Sara's Unisex Salon, Shitzu Squalo, Stock 305 clothing store, '67 Vapid Dominator Buggy and garage, Electric Fang tattoo parlour, One-Eyed Willie's mod shop, Goodtime Gear, PTT Youngin$ compound, Classic Car Collection). The Upgrade is also sold separately as a digital add-on on the PlayStation and Microsoft stores. Standard Edition owners, including physical-code owners, can buy it once their code has been redeemed. | ✅ (primary) | [65] |
| **No physical Ultimate Edition** | "A physical version of the Grand Theft Auto VI: Ultimate Edition is not available"; physical Standard owners can buy the Upgrade instead. | ✅ (primary) | [65] |
| Digital vs physical pre-order bonus | Digital pre-orders or purchases of either edition before **2026-11-20** get the Vintage Vice City Pack ('55 Vapid Stanier Sedan and garage, outfits and hairstyles, exclusive weapon pattern) **plus one month of GTA+ membership**. The physical code includes the Vintage Vice City Pack only, with no GTA+ month. Digital pre-load starts at local midnight on 2026-11-12; physical copies ship from 2026-11-12. | ✅ (primary) | [65] |
| Code region locks | Code redemption follows the region of the platform account. **PlayStation** codes are locked to these region groups: Brazil; LATAM (15 countries); North America (Canada, US); Korea; Japan; United Kingdom; Saudi Arabia & UAE; Rest of Asia (HK, Indonesia, Malaysia, Singapore, Taiwan, Thailand); Rest of EMEA and Asia (Australia, India, New Zealand, South Africa, Turkey, Ukraine and most European countries). **Xbox codes are not region-locked.** Japanese product codes expire 170 days after issuance, the issuance date being the 2026-11-19 launch ("due to regional regulations"). | ✅ (primary) | [65] |
| Monetisation | Rockstar stated no microtransactions at launch and no generative AI (per Wikipedia summary). | ⚠️ | [1] |
| Frame rate | 30 fps on consoles at launch (secondhand report). | ⚠️ | [23] |
| Build / version numbers | No retail build or day-one patch numbers published as of 2026-10-24. | — | not documented |
| GTA Online for GTA VI | No official announcement found. | — | not documented |

### 11.3 The September 2022 Rockstar Games network intrusion (GTA VI development-build leak)

| Field | Detail | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|
| Date posted | 18 September 2022 (Sunday), on GTAForums, user "teapotuberhacker" (PC Gamer dates the posts 19 Sept). | ✅ | [7][9][29] |
| Material | ~90 videos (~3 GB; ~50 min to "over an hour" of footage) of an early GTA VI development build; videos taken from Rockstar's internal Slack. A source told PC Gamer most clips were likely attached to QA bug reports. | ✅ | [7][8][9][29] |
| Visible build details | Placeholder/debug content: on-screen "Time Until Cops Dispatched" countdown; placeholder subtitle "Jason: GENERIC_CURSE_TO_SELF"; "WhatUp! message received" notification; recycled GTA V police car marked "V.C.P.D."; NPC clipping. Earlier leaked files carried "Americas" in their names, some showing the build running on a **PS4 dev kit**. | ✅ | [9] |
| Content | Vice City (Vice City Metro), playable female (Lucia) and male (Jason) protagonists, diner robbery, strip club, shootouts. | ✅ | [9][29] |
| Hacker claims | Claimed GTA V and GTA VI source code, assets and a test build; posted source-code snippets; threatened release ("start releasing the source code" if no contact within 24 h). | ✅ (claim) | [29][30] |
| Later leak of the stolen "Build" directory | Hidden Palace says the GTA V "Oct 13, 2014" development build (QA Version 108 / 0.0.0.77) comes from the 2022 leak's "Build" directory. That part was not published by the hacker but sold to private buyers, and an unknown buyer leaked it on **2026-09-18**, the fourth anniversary. It contains a very early GTA VI map rendition. See §8.1. | ⚠️ | [68] |
| Rockstar response | 19 Sept 2022 statement ("extremely disappointed"); Take-Two DMCA takedowns. Rockstar later told court the incident cost **US$5 million** and thousands of staff hours. | ✅ | [8][31] |
| Perpetrator | **Arion Kurtaj** (b. c.2005, Oxford), Lapsus$ member; carried out the hack from a Travelodge hotel where police had placed him while on bail, using an Amazon Fire TV Stick, hotel TV and phone; posted in Rockstar's Slack "I am not a Rockstar employee, I am an attacker." | ✅ | [30][31][32] |
| Arrest | 22 September 2022 (City of London Police). | ✅ | [1] |
| Trial | Southwark Crown Court, ~7-week trial, 2023. Found unfit to stand trial (autism); jury found on **23 August 2023** that he had committed all 12 alleged acts (incl. Rockstar, Uber, Nvidia, BT/EE, Revolut). | ✅ | [30][32] |
| Sentence | December 2023 (reported 21–22 Dec 2023): indefinite hospital order (secure hospital). | ✅ | [31][32] |
| 2026 developments | Moved from secure hospital to prison, reported by BBC's Joe Tidy on 2026-07-14; assessed fit to plead; criminal trial set for **9 November 2026 at Woolwich Crown Court** (per defence chambers). | ✅ | [33][34] |

### 11.3a GTA VI development builds and clips in the September 2022 leak (from the "Leakless" leak analysis)

Everything in this subsection comes from one community document: **"Comprehensive GTA VI Leak Analysis" v1.0**, released under the "Leakless" name and compiled "with help from the extended mapping and r/GTA6 discord communities as well as GTAForums" [70]. No second source was found for the build numbers, so every row is ⚠️ (one community source). Values are copied as the document gives them. Its MM/DD/YYYY dates are converted to ISO, and no number has been corrected or filled in.

**What the leak contained, according to [70]:**

| Item | Detail as given in [70] | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|
| Video clips | 90 clips, from under 10 seconds to over 3 minutes long, recorded March 2021 to September 2022. Filenames use the project codename **"Americas"** in two forms, `Americas YYYY-MM-DD HH-MM-SS` and `Americas_1 YYYY-MM-DD HH-MM-SS`. The document does not explain the `_1` suffix. It refers to 58 clips by filename (listed in the second table below). | ⚠️ | [70] |
| Python code | `GTA6MENU.py`, a menu script rather than game code, containing the comment "#americas is purple background with white text" (hex `#800080`). | ⚠️ | [70] |
| C++ code | "GameHeaders_Tasks": AI task code that "specifically referred to GTA V features". | ⚠️ | [70] |
| Other data | GTA V vehicle handling data from GTA V's source code. | ⚠️ | [70] |
| PDF screenshots | 2 screenshots of an "Americas" PDF about camera controls and build previews for developers. The build shown is listed as **"PDF Screenshot v270.0"**, the lowest build number in the document, with no date. | ⚠️ | [70] |
| Executables | Clips dated 12 and 13 Sep 2022 carry `game_win64_bankrelease.exe` in their file metadata tags. Other clips show `game_win64_beta.exe`, and one shows `game_win64_debug.exe`. The document reads "bankrelease" as aiming at "feature complete" and "beta" as aiming at "content complete"; that is its own interpretation. | ⚠️ | [70] |
| Sample clip metadata | Quoted for one `Americas_1` clip dated 2022-09-13: recorded with Microsoft Game DVR; `startTime` 2022-09-13T15:24:00Z, `endTime` 2022-09-13T15:24:43Z, 43 seconds; `game_win64_bankrelease.exe`; Intel Core i9-10980XE @ 3.00 GHz; NVIDIA GeForce RTX 2080 SUPER, driver 31.0.15.1659; VMware Horizon Indirect Display Driver 1.4.18.0. | ⚠️ | [70] |
| PCs used for recordings | 13 CPU/GPU combinations: Xeon E5-1650 v3 + GTX 1080; Xeon W-2135 + GTX 1080; Threadripper 3970X + RTX 2080 SUPER; Threadripper PRO 3975WX + RTX 2080 SUPER; Threadripper 2950X + GTX TITAN X; Xeon W-2135 + RTX 2070 SUPER; Xeon W-2135 + RTX 2080 SUPER; Threadripper PRO 3955WX + RTX 3080; Core i9-10980XE + RTX 2080 SUPER; Threadripper PRO 3955WX + RTX 2080 SUPER; Threadripper PRO 3975WX + RTX 3060 Ti; Threadripper PRO 3955WX + RTX 3060 Ti; Threadripper PRO 3975WX + RTX 3080. | ⚠️ | [70] |
| PS4 dev kit | Under "Useful Links", the document labels psdevwiki's page for PS4 system software **5.508.011** as "The PS4 Dev Kit used in the leaks" (https://www.psdevwiki.com/ps4/5.508.011). | ⚠️ | [70] |
| Engine / debug details | RAGE with RDR2's lighting and volumetric-cloud skybox; a C++ user-interface library the document names "Dear ImUI". Debug character labels `PLAYER_LUCIA_PLAYER_STORY000_00` and `PLAYER_JASON_PLAYER_STORY000_01`, numbered by story stage. World Events show **Passing**/**NotPassing** rule states (e.g. *isPlayableCharacter*, *isWantedRule*). Some clips use low-detail `[low_int]` character models. The document also lists 190 "Rage AI Debug Options" menu entries (e.g. HorseAvoidance, DecisionMaker, Stealth, Robbery, Jacking). | ⚠️ | [70] |
| Original leak thread | Linked in the document as GTAForums topic 985481, "GTA 6 Americas leak 90 mp4 footage/videos" (archived): https://gtaforums.com/topic/985481-gta-6-americas-leak-90-mp4-footagevideos/ | ⚠️ | [70] |

**Build versions by date.** This is every entry in the document's "Development Builds" list, in its order. The document does not say where each number was read from. **Things the list itself shows** (taken directly from [70], not explanations):
- **Out of order numbers.** On several dates the build number is lower than on an earlier date: 2022-01-11 (v470.0, after v470.0.7), 2022-05-20 (v504.0, after v506.0.8), 2022-06-14 (v510.0, after v510.0.4), 2022-08-11 (v528.0, after v531.0.4) and 2022-09-13 (v533.0, after v542.0). The document gives no explanation, and none is offered here.
- **Clips with no listed build.** The document names clips from 18 dates that are not in its build list:
  - 2021-05-17 (`Americas_1 … 16-38-34`, extended cover)
  - 2021-07-09 (`Americas … 15-18-02`, the Bayfront Park amphitheatre location)
  - 2021-09-10 (`Americas_1 … 16-37-50`: weapon wheel like 2021-06-22, dropping/picking up weapons, the Margaret Pace Park location and the Biscayne Bay / Venetian Causeway view)
  - 2021-12-14 (`Americas_1 … 19-52-44`, Jason and Lucia's motel clip; Humble Pie, "30 Days in the Hole", at 0:28)
  - 2022-04-13 (`Americas_1 … 09-08-46`, NPCs flee holding phones; `Americas_1 … 17-42-41`, debug inputs `INPUT_HORSE_EXIT` and `INPUT_VEH_EXIT`)
  - 2022-05-05 (`Americas_1 … 10-00-47`, diving animations like San Andreas)
  - 2022-05-24 (`Americas_1 … 17-19-42`, multiplayer: one avatar carries another's body, `CARRY SLOT<0>[HUMAN_CHEST]`, "PL: 2/32", "SH: PR_RSN1", "GM H: PR_RSN1")
  - 2022-06-01 (`Americas … 17-16-15`, testbed animation clip; newest weapon wheel with no dual-wielding, quick inventory at centre-left, cigarette icon)
  - 2022-06-17, 2022-07-08 and 2022-07-13 (`… 17-14-50`, `… 19-10-17`, `… 16-47-50`: dithered fade-in as objects load)
  - 2022-08-02 (`Americas_1 … 18-34-23`, Lucia's alternate look; `Americas_1 … 20-20-00`, Lucia firing from the back of a truck in a police chase, a Krome Ave, Homestead–based location and a "Keys Causeway" sign)
  - 2022-08-10 (`Americas_1 … 12-27-03`, injured tip: "Your Health will regenerate slowly, open your Weapon Wheel and use a recovery item…")
  - 2022-08-19 (`Americas_1 … 14-40-45`, Lucia in a club with a "Sinful Sundays" poster; Egyptian Lover, "Everything She Wants", and ScHoolboy Q, "Numb Numb Juice", on iFruit Radio)
  - 2022-08-23 (`Americas_1 … 15-56-16`, an `AmbientCollectibleHat`)
  - 2022-08-24 (`Americas_1 … 07-48-28`, a `Collectibles_Car_Parts` / `WymanCarPartsBoxedGenericUsed` box with the Classique logo)
  - 2022-08-29 (`Americas_1 … 12-44-36`, debug `Interior:157 … ss15lok_1_gfa_bespoke`, so at least 157 interiors; a Grassrivers wildlife placard reading "A new alpha"; Youth Brigade, "Blown Away" (Channel X), at 0:09)
  - 2022-09-01 (`Americas_1 … 14-37-21`, Jason aims a rifle out of a car window at tennis courts, at the Margaret Pace Park location)
- **Listed builds with no named clip.** 17 dated entries have no clip named in the document: 2021-03-03, 04-15, 05-18, 05-28, 06-04, 11-05, 11-23; 2022-01-11, 03-11, 03-21, 03-23, 04-05, 04-27, 06-08, 08-05, 08-08 and 08-11. The document names only 58 of the 90 clips.

### 11.4 December 2023 GTA VI Trailer 1 leak

| Field | Detail | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|
| Date | 4 December 2023 (evening, US time) — ~15 hours before the planned 2023-12-05 09:00 ET premiere. | ✅ | [3][5] |
| Nature | Low-quality copy of the official trailer circulated on X/Twitter; Rockstar responded by publishing the full trailer immediately. Viewed ~60 million times on YouTube by next morning (Axios); ~101 million within two days (Wikipedia). | ✅ | [3][5] |
| Source of leak | Not officially disclosed. | — | not documented |

### 11.5 2026 "CyberLeek" GTA VI leaks (pre-launch)

| Date (2026) | Event | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|
| 08-14 / 08-15 | "CyberLeek" website, backup domains and a Solana-based **$CYBERLEEK** meme token reportedly set up. | ⚠️ | [20] |
| 08-18 | First gameplay clips published (basketball, roadside confrontation) with a "consumer-rights" manifesto (against digital-only/code-in-box physical release, paid edition content, and for keeping single-player games playable after support ends); alleged Leonida map images circulate. | ✅ | [20][21][62] |
| 08-19 → 08-25 | Further clips (taser, "Junkies", plane clip with "LEEK" shot into a wall — read as evidence of hands-on access to a playable build; club, beach, store, death screen, first on-screen Lucia mention); token-gated polls; fake "leaked build" torrents circulate. Wikipedia counts at least 16 videos over nine days by 26 Aug. | ✅ / ⚠️ (counts) | [1][20] |
| 08-21 onward | Take-Two legal action reportedly targets Microsoft and Discord (subpoenas to identify accounts); Wikipedia also lists X Corp. Xbox CTO publicly said Microsoft was cooperating. | ✅ | [1][20][61] |
| 08-26 | Rockstar statement: leaks "heartbreaking for our team"; game nearly finished; Extended Look still premiering 27 Aug; release date reaffirmed. | ✅ | [21] |
| 08-27 | Media reported CyberLeek withdrew ~US$268,000 in token fees and the token collapsed (per Wikipedia). | ⚠️ | [1] |
| Build provenance | Never established publicly; no playable build distributed. Suggested late-stage build (SOCRadar analysis). | ❓ | [20] |
| Identity | Not publicly identified as of 2026-10-24 (no arrest found in sources consulted). | — | not documented |

**Other GTA VI leak-related events:** October 2025 dismissal of 34 Rockstar staff for alleged leaking (see 11.1) ✅ [1][13]. No other leaked GTA VI *build* is documented.

### 11.6 December 2023 GTA V source-code leak

| Field | Detail | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|
| Date | ~24 December 2023 (first public claim on X by @SKizzleAXE with a screenshot; Bully 2 file screenshot shared 25 Dec). Came days after Kurtaj's sentencing. | ✅ | [37][39][40] |
| Origin | Reportedly first shared in a Discord server; full contents reposted via r/GamingLeaksAndRumours. Possibly linked to Lapsus$ members (Wikipedia, hedged). Whether it derives from the 2022 intrusion is unconfirmed. | ⚠️ | [39][40] |
| Contents (reported) | GTA V source code; an early GTA V map and script; references to **"Project Americas"** (GTA VI codename); a GTA VI PC script / code snippets incl. a lock-picking mechanic; Bully 2 concepts/file; material purportedly from the cancelled PS3 game *Agent*. | ✅ (reported by multiple outlets) | [37][38][39] |
| Cancelled GTA V single-player DLC names found | **SP Assassination Pack**, **SP Manhunt Pack**, **SP Norman Pack**, **Agent Trevor**, **Relationship Pack**, **Enterprise Pack**, **Prologue DLC**, **LibertyV DLC** (Liberty City). Descriptions beyond names are journalist speculation. Wikipedia: at least eight SP DLC sets in development, scrapped as the team focused on GTA Online and RDR2. | ✅ (names) / ❓ (content) | [37][38][40] |
| Rockstar response | No public statement found. | — | [37][39] |
| Build number of the leaked code | Hidden Palace catalogues the leaked source as the PC "Sep 16, 2022 build", build name **1.0.2699.0**, code from about 2022-09-16, released by "Lapsus$". It is code only (assets come from a retail copy); Rockstar's build tools are included, and so far only the "Debug" configuration is known to work. | ⚠️ (single community source) | [68] |
| Pre-release GTA V *builds* | *(Corrected: an earlier draft said none with documented build numbers had leaked.)* Hidden Palace documents a GTA V development build dated **2014-10-13** (GTA5_NG QA Version 108 / 0.0.0.77). It came from the "Build" directory of the September 2022 intrusion and leaked publicly on **2026-09-18**. Hidden Palace also documents PS3 patch prototypes (builds 377 and 388.5), Xbox 360 PartnerNet DLC prototypes (2013) and a 2020 "GTA5 Debug.exe". See §8.1 and 11.7. | ⚠️ | [68] |

### 11.8 Cancelled, unreleased & lost GTA projects (series-wide)

| Project | Platform / type | Period | What is documented | Conf. | Src / see |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| *Race'n'Chase* (working title of GTA) | PC etc. | 1995–97 | Original concept/title of GTA; early prototypes released by Mike Dailly. Not a cancellation — it became GTA. | ✅ | §1, [47] |
| GTA — Sega Saturn version | Saturn | c.1997 | Halted early (weak sourcing: Wikipedia "citation needed", GTA Wiki). | ⚠️ | §1 |
| GTA — Nintendo 64 port ("Grand Theft Auto 64") | N64 | 1999 | Cancelled during DMA ownership changes (Retro Gamer via Wikipedia; IGN 29 Mar 1999). | ✅ | §1, [50] |
| GTA / GTA 2 — Palm OS ports | Palm OS | 2001–02 | Announced by Take-Two 26 Mar 2001 (Global Star Software); demos released; cancelled by mid-2002. | ✅ / ⚠️ | §1, §2 |
| GTA 2 — Miami project: "expansion" vs ***Grand Theft Auto 2.5*** ("GTA Miami") | PC | c.1999–2000 | **Two descriptions that conflict.** (a) Wikipedia, citing the book *Grand Thieves & Tomb Raiders*: DMA's Dundee office worked on a Miami-set GTA 2 *expansion*, dropped as the Edinburgh 3D project (GTA III) took priority. (b) GTA Wiki, citing Obbe Vermeij's blog post of 2023-11-11: the project was ***Grand Theft Auto 2.5***, also called the "GTA Miami" project. It was a separate game, named "2.5" because it would follow GTA 2 and used an isometric top-down 3D ("2.5-dimensional", SimCity-like) view. It was set in "Miami" and was cancelled when DMA's Dundee office closed. Vermeij's blog post itself could not be read on 2026-10-24 (Blogger returned "page … does not exist"), so (b) rests on GTA Wiki's citation. | ✅ (a: book via Wikipedia) / ⚠️ (b: wiki citing blog) / conflict on "expansion" vs separate game | [50][67] |
| *Grand Theft Auto: Online Crime World* | PC (online) | c.1999–2000 | In development at DMA at Take-Two's acquisition; abandoned with the dropped Broadband Studios merger (Guardian, PC Zone, Time Extension via Wikipedia). | ✅ | [50] |
| GTA III — Dreamcast development | Dreamcast | 1999–2000 | Tech demo and ~4 months of development on Dreamcast before moving to PS2; Obbe Vermeij: Dreamcast not commercially viable. No build preserved. (A fan re3-based Dreamcast port appeared 2024 — unofficial.) | ✅ | [49], §3 |
| GTA III — online multiplayer | PS2 | 2000–01 | Planned online mode dropped for time/resources. | ✅ | [49] |
| ***Grand Theft Auto: Online*** ("GTA:O"): MMO proposal by **Barking Dog Studios** (later Rockstar Vancouver) | PC (RenderWare, per GTA Wiki infobox) | c. May 2001 | An MMORPG concept pitched to Rockstar Games around May 2001, to be made with Rockstar North: a persistent online cops-and-robbers world with player-created characters. Evidence: the *Grand Theft Auto: Online* Design Document **Version 1.20, dated 31 May 2001** (Barking Dog Studios), which researchers made public in 2024 after getting it from an unnamed Rockstar employee. Obbe Vermeij posted on X on 2024-12-25 that three Barking Dog staff visited Edinburgh and that "None of this was greenlit or actually worked on". It is **not** the GTA III online mode in the row above. Since 2024 outlets have mislabelled it "GTA III Online", because the design document uses a GTA III testbed screenshot as a placeholder. | ⚠️ (wiki citing the design doc and Vermeij's post) | [66] |
| GTA III — Xbox exclusive offer | Xbox | 2001 | Rockstar offered GTA III to Microsoft as an Xbox exclusive; declined. (Later released on Xbox 2003.) | ✅ | [49] |
| GTA III / Vice City — GameCube port | GameCube | c.2003 | Expected by analysts; never materialised. | ⚠️ | [49], §3 |
| "Z" — zombie survival game | PS2 | c. late 2002 | Rockstar North project on a Scottish island reusing Vice City code; dropped after ~1 month (Obbe Vermeij via Eurogamer/RPS, cited by Wikipedia). Not GTA-branded. | ✅ | [50] |
| GTA III GBA port by Crawfish Interactive (original *GTA Advance*) | GBA | 2002 | Started as a GTA III port by Crawfish; tech demo dated 2002-04-16 survives; Crawfish closed Nov 2002; game rebuilt by Digital Eclipse and released Oct 2004. No Rockstar Leeds involvement documented. | ✅ | [41][57], §6 |
| *Agent* (Rockstar North) | PS3 | 2007–2018 | Teased E3 2007, announced 2 Jun 2009 as PS3 exclusive; Cold War spy setting; staff moved to GTA V; USPTO trademark declared abandoned 19 Nov 2018; site redirected by Oct 2021. Material purportedly from *Agent* appeared in the Dec 2023 leak. Not GTA, listed for cross-reference. | ✅ | [52][39] |
| GTA IV — cut features | X360/PS3 | 2007–08 | In-game record shop/MP3 player cut; SA-style RPG elements removed; co-op story campaign considered and rejected. | ✅ | [63] |
| GTA IV — zombie mode "Z: Resurrection" | X360 | 2007 | Referenced in the Nov 2007 Xbox 360 prototype. | ⚠️ | §7 |
| GTA IV — third episode / further episodes | X360 | 2009 | No planned third episode found in Wikipedia's GTA IV / EFLC articles. Microsoft paid US$50m for the two episodes. | — | **not documented**; [54][63] |
| GTA V — cancelled single-player DLC (8 sets) | PS3/X360/PS4… | c.2013–2015 | Names from 2023 source leak: SP Assassination Pack, SP Manhunt Pack, SP Norman Pack, **Agent Trevor**, Relationship Pack, Enterprise Pack, Prologue DLC, **LibertyV** (Liberty City). Scrapped to focus on GTA Online & RDR2. | ✅ (names) | 11.6, [37][38][40] |
| GTA V — "alien/zombie" story DLC | — | — | No documentation found linking a specific alien or zombie SP DLC to GTA V beyond the names above. | ❓ | not documented |
| *GTA Tokyo* | — | — | No reliable source found in the material consulted (not mentioned in Wikipedia articles on GTA, Rockstar North, Rockstar Leeds). | ❓ | not documented |
| *GTA: Liberty City Stories* early concepts | PSP | 2005–06 | No separate concept or cancelled version found. Hidden Palace documents nine LCS pre-release builds, from 2005-05-29 to 2006-03-22 (see 11.7 and §6.2.1); all are builds of the released game. Multiplayer was on PSP but removed from the PS2 and mobile versions. | ✅ (prototypes) | [42][58], (§6) |
| *GTA: Vice City Online* | — | — | No evidence found. | — | not documented |
| "GTA: Online Bonus Pack" | — | — | No evidence found. | — | not documented |
| Official J2ME / feature-phone GTA | Mobile | — | No official Java/J2ME GTA found; official mobile releases are iOS/Android/Fire OS/Windows Phone ports (GTA III, VC, SA, LCS, CW). | — | not documented; [53] |
| Unofficial J2ME ".jar" games named "GTA" | J2ME (CLDC/MIDP) | — | libretro's J2ME DAT lists GTA-named jars. The vendor named in each filename's parentheses is never Rockstar or Take-Two: **"GTA (Auer Media)"** v1.20.02 / v1.32.02 (3 dumps) and **"GTA_lo (Auer Media)"** v1.20.02 (2 dumps); **"GTA Vice city (Tag Games Limited)"** v0.2.2; **"GTA IV Mobile [sefan.ru] (Tag Games Limited)"** v0.2.15; **"GTA Soviet Russia (F.M.R)"** v0.2.15 / v1.1.0 and "(Tag Games, F.M.R)" v0.2.2 / v0.2.3; **"GTA4 BY DJ27 (DJ27 MASTER-T)"** v1.0.9. The DAT has no publisher or year field. The "[sefan.ru]" tag (a download-site tag), the "F.M.R" and "DJ27" credits, and Tag Games and F.M.R jars that share version numbers all suggest unofficial or bootleg remakes; that is an inference, not stated by the DAT. These jars support, and do not contradict, the conclusion above that no official J2ME GTA exists. | ⚠️ (single list; status inferred from the vendor names) | [69] |
| *Chinatown Wars* for iPod (click-wheel) | iPod | — | No evidence found (the game was released on iOS — see §6). | — | not documented |
| GTA film adaptation | Film | — | No official GTA film produced; Take-Two states it owns film rights and will act against any film titled *Grand Theft Auto* (Wikipedia; Roger Corman's 1977 film *Grand Theft Auto* is unrelated). | ⚠️ | [53] |
| Rockstar Leeds cancelled projects | — | 2000–2010 | Non-GTA: *Titanium Angels* (PS2), *Necroscope* (PS2/Xbox), *The Warriors* GBA, *Bully* DS. No cancelled GTA project at Rockstar Leeds documented. | ✅ | [51] |

### 11.10 Gaps & caveats (Section 11)

- GTA VI is **unreleased** at research date (2026-10-24): no retail build numbers, day-one patch, PC version or GTA Online-for-VI details exist publicly.
- **GTA VI development build numbers (§11.3a)** come from one community document [70]. A web search found no second source listing the same numbers, and the document does not say where each number was read from. Treat them as ⚠️ until a second source confirms them. The 32 leaked clips the document does not name are not covered.
- Web-search quota was exhausted partway through research; the following could not be further verified and are flagged accordingly: GTA Tokyo, any GTA IV third episode, alien/zombie GTA V DLC, official J2ME GTA, PEGI/USK/CERO ratings, CyberLeek identity.
- TCRF prototype pages for GTA 2, London 1969 and Vice City, and GTA Wiki (Fandom) pages, could not be retrieved (site errors); per-game sections hold what was found.
- 30 fps frame-rate claim for GTA VI is secondhand (YouTuber account), not a Rockstar statement.
- **Audit integration (2026-10-24):** added the GTA VI Ultimate Edition Upgrade, the GTA+ month for digital pre-orders, the lack of a physical Ultimate Edition and the PlayStation code region locks (all from Rockstar Support); the Barking Dog *GTA: Online* proposal (2001); the "GTA 2.5" description, shown next to the Miami "expansion" wording as a conflict; the unofficial J2ME jars; the 1.0.2699.0 source build; and the leaked GTA V Oct 13, 2014 build. The stale §11.6 statement is corrected, and the 11.7 index was rebuilt from Sections 1–8. Still open: Obbe Vermeij's 2023-11-11 blog post (the primary source for "GTA 2.5") could not be loaded, and the Barking Dog design document itself was not seen, only GTA Wiki's citation of it.

## 12. Compilations, Collections, Bundles & Special Editions

Scope: every multi-game compilation, bundle, "special/collector's/complete" edition and console-hardware bundle of Grand Theft Auto titles. Single-game platform ports, budget reprints of a single game and expansions are covered in their own sections; they appear here only where they were sold as part of a bundle or as a named special edition. Confidence key: ✅ confirmed by an official or multiple independent sources; ⚠️ single community/database source, or sources conflict; ❓ rumour/unverified. "Not documented" means no source was found during this research pass — not that the item does not exist.

The only documented "Rockstar Games Collection: Edition 1" is the 2012 PS3/Xbox 360 North American release above; contemporaneous reports said a PC or European version was unconfirmed [40]. No 2006 PC release under that name was found.
- An Xbox 360 hardware bundle with GTA IV + Episodes from Liberty City is widely remembered but was **not verified** in this pass. ❓

### 12.7 Regional and other items not verified

| Item from brief | Status |
|---|---|
| Japanese "Grand Theft Auto Triple Pack" / Capcom compilation | Not documented. The documented Japanese compilations are Capcom's **Grand Theft Auto Double Pack** for Xbox (29 Jul 2004, A4K-00001) and PS2 (22 Feb 2007, CPCS-01027) [19][61], plus the Japanese Complete Edition (23 Jun 2011) and Trilogy Ketteiban (27 Jan 2022 physical) [64][67]. |
| Korean compilation editions | No Korean compilation found. GameFAQs lists Korean releases of GTA IV: The Complete Edition (PS3 9 Dec 2010, X360 13 Dec 2010, PC 7 Dec 2011) [64]. Redump lists a Korean PSP LCS "Greatest Hits" (ULKS-46157) [68]. |
| German "Gold Edition" / Australian bundles | No German "Gold Edition" found. Related finds: the German Xbox "Rockstar Games Doppelpack" (§12.2), Poland's "GTA2: Złota Edycja" ("Gold Edition", single game, PC; redump) [68] and the Australian PS1 "Double Feature" (§12.1) [58]. |
| GTA Mega Collection, Best of Rockstar, Rockstar Classics retail compilation | Not documented as retail compilations. "Rockstar Classics" is documented as the free PC download series (§12.1) and, separately, as a Japanese budget label (§12.6). |
| Steam "Grand Theft Auto Complete Pack" / "Rockstar Complete Pack" | Not verified. The current Steam store lists no GTA bundle except the GTA V Enhanced editions. The delisted Steam apps (GTA III 12100, VC 12110, SA 12120, EFLC 12220, GTA V Legacy 271590) return no package data from the Steam store API, and steamdb.info answered with a bot check. GameFAQs gives Steam ID 11902 for the PC/Mac Trilogy (6 Jan 2011) [62]. *Update (audit pass):* a 2013 Steam "Grand Theft Auto Complete Pack" (sub 18832) is now partly documented; see the follow-up row below. |
| **Steam "Grand Theft Auto Complete Pack"** (follow-up) | **Partly verified.** Cowcotland (2013-04-28): a Steam bundle at €12.49 with seven games: GTA, GTA 2, GTA III, Vice City, San Andreas, GTA IV and Episodes from Liberty City. A Steam Community thread (opened 2013-12-27) has a buyer who bought it on 2013-03-29 and got GTA 1 and 2 under the package name "GTA Complete Pack (ROW)", linking SteamDB **sub 18832**. Steam's packagedetails API returns `success:false` for 18832 (delisted). MobyGames separately lists "Grand Theft Auto: Complete Pack" (2010, Windows) and "Grand Theft Auto Complete Package" (2013, Windows). Whether those are this Steam bundle is **not documented**; the dates differ. [77][78][79] ⚠️ |
| MobyGames-only listings (audit §14.2) | Seen only in the MobyGames "Grand Theft Auto series" group (Wayback capture 2026-01-10): **"Grand Theft Auto: Collection" (2008, Windows)** and **"Grand Theft Auto: Online – Shark: Extortionate Value" (2015, Windows)**. MobyGames was behind a Cloudflare check on 2026-10-24, so the game pages could not be read. A GameFAQs search for "grand theft auto collection / complete / pack" and "shark extortionate" found neither, and a web search for "Extortionate Value" returned only the current Microsoft Store shark-card bundles. Contents, IDs and exact dates are **not documented**. [79][80] ⚠️ (listing only) |
| GTA V "Game of the Year"-style edition | No such edition found; GTA V's later editions are the Premium Online Edition / Premium Edition (§12.4). |
| Xbox One / Xbox 360 GTA V console bundles; Xbox 360 GTA IV + EFLC console bundle | ❓ Widely reported but not verified with a source in this pass. The PS3 (2013) and PS4 (US 2014, Japan 2014) GTA V bundles are now sourced (§12.4). |
| "Grand Theft Auto: The Collection" / "The Grand Theft Auto Collection" (PS1) | Not documented under those names (§12.1 note). |

## 13. Country-specific Releases, Censorship & Localisation

Cross-game view of what differs per country/region. Confidence: ✅ confirmed by an official/primary or multiple independent sources · ⚠️ single community source (wiki/forum/fan cut report) · ❓ rumour / unverified. "Not documented" = searched, nothing found. Src numbers refer to `#### Sources (Section 13)` at the end.

### 13.1 Germany (USK / BPjS-BPjM indexing)

**System notes.** German releases from GTA III onwards were typically edited to obtain a USK 16 rating ("Deutsche Version"). On the first PS2/PC editions of GTA III and Vice City the cuts were tied to the German language setting — switching the console/PC to another language gave the uncut game; later German releases removed this loophole [S3][S5]. A Vice City PS2 first edition was indexed by the Bundesprüfstelle für jugendgefährdende Schriften (BPjS) in March 2003 precisely because the uncut English version was reachable through the language setting [S4]. Foreign (Austrian/Swiss/UK) versions have no region lock and play uncut on German systems [S6].

**Indexing summary (Germany).** Documented BPjS/BPjM listings: Vice City (first PS2 edition, Mar 2003; delisted Oct 2014, re-rated uncut USK 18 Dec 2014); Vice City Stories (EU PS2, Nov 2007; delisted Oct 2015); Liberty City Stories (EU PS2, Dec 2007; delisted Sep 2015); San Andreas (EU PC, 7 Jan 2009; delisting session 3 Sep 2015). Schnittberichte headline "Drei Grand Theft Auto-Teile rehabilitiert" ("Three GTA parts rehabilitated", news 9573) and "Nach Listenstreichung: GTA-Teile unzensiert geprüft" (ticker 2118) refer to a delisting/uncut re-rating batch — page bodies could not be fetched; the 2015 trio (SA, LCS, VCS) plausibly matches "three parts", but this is ❓ unverified. GTA III was *not* indexed according to Schnittberichte's VC report [S5].

### 13.2 Japan (CERO; Syscom / Capcom / CyberFront / Take-Two Japan)

**Background.** Rockstar/Take-Two chose Capcom for GTA III in 2003 over the smaller publishers that had handled GTA 1 and 2 (Time Extension names Syscom Entertainment and Zoo Corporation) [S23]. Japan had no mature rating until CERO (est. June 2002); CERO later introduced the adults-only "Z" rating; grandtheftwiki states all GTA games in Japan were rated CERO Z/18 [S23][S25]. Kanagawa Prefecture's child-welfare council moved on 30 May 2005 to designate GTA III a "harmful" product (no sale/rental under 18, separate display) — first such action in Japan over violence (not sex); approved the following month [S23][S26]. Game Developer (Aug 2008) states two of Japan's 47 prefectures banned GTA III (PS2) from sale [S28].

### 13.3 Australia (OFLC / Classification Board; no R18+ for games until 2013 [S18])

Rockstar statement (4 Feb 2008, quoted by Refused-Classification): "No GTA has been censored since VICE CITY" — reversed in April 2008 when it confirmed an edited GTA IV [S43c]. Edits were performed by the distributor/developer before submission; the Board stated (6 May 2008) "The board did not make any changes" [S43c].

| Game | Platform | Release / version | Date | Differences / cuts / rating | Publisher/distributor | Conf. | Src |

| GTA III | PS2 | Original release (shipped before classification, expecting MA) | late 2001 | Classification Board 28 Nov 2001: **Refused Classification** — majority linked beating a prostitute after sex to "sexualised violence". ### 13.15 Known gaps / unresolved items (Section 13)

- **Schnittberichte pages not readable**: in the first pass, server errors and then the fetch quota stopped access. In the second pass, schnittberichte.com returned Cloudflare "521: Web server is down" for every page tried (24 Oct 2026, 03:06–03:10 UTC). Affected pages: GTA 1 USK 16 report (ID 3164), GTA III report (ID 3239), London 1969 (ID 3165), GTA IV Australian-version report (ID 984017), "Drei GTA-Teile rehabilitiert" (news 9573), "Nach Listenstreichung…" (ticker 2118), "GTA 4 und die USK" (news 1014), "GTA: Chinatown Wars kommt unzensiert" (news 1223), and the rejected GTA IV indexing application (special 224). Whether the German GTA IV was cut is still the biggest open question.
- **Japan:** the GTA V Japanese cut list beyond the torture-gameplay removal. The Japanese corporate publisher of GTA V and EFLC: GameFAQs says only "Rockstar Games", and Take-Two Interactive Japan is not confirmed. The GTA 2 Japanese publisher and release date: the Zoo link is an inference, and GameFAQs lists no Japanese GTA 2. GTA 1 (PC: Zoo, 23 Jan 1998; PS1: Syscom, 27 Aug 1998) and the Chinatown Wars platforms (DS and PSP, CyberFront) are now documented.
- **Russia/CIS:** Vice City's Russian release date. The GTA IV "Russian version" patch 1.0.4.2 (not documented). Noviy Disk: no GTA title found in the sources read. Publishers per game are now documented from ru.wiki (Buka, 1C-SoftClub, SoftClub, 1C).
- **Poland/Czech/Italy/Spain/France:** Polish (CD Projekt, Play-It, Cenega), Czech (Cenega, GTA III only) and French (Take-Two) distributors are now listed from wiki infoboxes and are single-source ⚠️. Italian and Spanish distributors are not documented, and local content differences are not documented.
- **Venezuela:** no GTA-specific ban found. **Mainland China:** no official release found. **Hong Kong/Taiwan:** no Asia-English edition SKUs documented beyond GTA V's Traditional Chinese text (GameFAQs lists Asian GTA V PS3 BLAS-50639 and PS4 PLAS-07010 SKUs, Section 12 sources).
- **Brazil:** ClassInd ratings are not documented. The EFLC injunction is dated 18 Oct 2010 (Kotaku), but Canaltech says 2013. The lift is dated "later in 2010" (Terra) or 2012 (Wikipedia), while Canaltech says the outcome was never made public, so no lift date is documented. The 2026 Digital ECA store suspension has two possible start dates (16 vs 17 Mar 2026 for the law taking effect).
- **Saudi Arabia/UAE:** the 17 Jul 2025 approval with a 21+ rating is now press-sourced. The start date and scope of the original bans are still uncited.
- **South Korea:** the dates and a citation for the GTA III/VC bans could not be found. A Korean-language search (GameMeca, brunch) gave nothing on a ban. ko.wiki contradicts itself on which game was the "first official Korean localisation" (VC mobile 2012 vs GTA V 2013).

## 14. Completeness Audit Notes

> Independent audit pass carried out on 2026-10-24 after sections 1–13 and 15 were drafted. This section does **not** change sections 1–13. It lists items that those sections leave out, and errors in them, so they can be fixed. Every row below was checked against at least one source (cited by number). Confidence markers follow the header legend.

### 14.1 Method & master lists used

1. **Series and platform master lists:** the English Wikipedia *Grand Theft Auto* series article (the games tables for main series, expansions, handhelds, compilations) [1]. Each title and platform was checked against the section files with keyword and serial greps.
2. **Prototype / beta builds:** Hidden Palace. Its MediaWiki API was queried for the terms "Grand Theft Auto", "GTA", "Liberty City", "Vice City", "San Andreas", "Chinatown Wars", "Rockstar North" and "DMA Design" [2]. That gave 119+ pages. Redirects were resolved to their canonical pages, then the build date of each page was looked up in sections 1–13.
3. **Retail disc / digital dumps:** every serial or content ID in `/home/claude/gta_dat_entries.md` was extracted (214 unique IDs from the libretro Redump, No-Intro and "dat" mirrors [12][13][14][15]) and searched for in sections 1–13, allowing for hyphenated and unhyphenated forms. Variants that are only a box-code suffix of a serial already present (for example `SLES-50330-P`, `SLUS-20062GH`) count as present. Appendix A (section 15) was checked as well.
4. **Other master lists:** see 14.4 for GTA Wiki, MobyGames, GTA Online updates and mobile platforms.

### 14.2 Items missing from sections 1–13 (verified)

| Item | Belongs in section | Details (dates/IDs) | Conf. | Src |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **GTA V "Oct 13, 2014" prototype (GTA5_NG QA Version 108 / version 0.0.0.77)**: full multi-platform development build | §8.1 (leaked builds) + §11.7 index | Build date 2014-10-13. Hidden Palace lists the file release date as **2026-09-18** ("four year anniversary of the 2022 leaks"). It came from the "Build" directory taken in the Sept 2022 intrusion, was sold privately, and was then leaked by an unknown buyer. Binaries and baked assets for all platforms; debug keyboards. Contains a very early rendition of the GTA VI map (`-level env_test`). Hidden Palace says this shows Cayo Perico was partly cut GTA VI content. Also has assets for a cut Liberty City expansion and the cut "Agent Trevor" DLC. Map files last exported c. 2014-12-19. §8.1 currently says no pre-release GTA V build was found; §11.6 says "none … leaked". Both are now out of date. | ⚠️ (single community source; recent) | [3] |
| **GTA V "Sep 16, 2022 build" (source-code build 1.0.2699.0)** | §11.6 / §11.7 | Hidden Palace treats the leaked GTA V source as a PC "prototype" with build name **1.0.2699.0** and code dated about **2022-09-16**, released by "Lapsus$". It comes with Rockstar's own build tools, contains code only (assets must be copied from a retail copy), and only the Debug configuration is known to compile. This ties the Dec 2023 source leak to a specific build number, which §11.6 does not give. | ⚠️ | [4] |
| GTA V Xbox 360 **PartnerNet DLC prototypes** (2013) | §8.1 / §9.A (cut SP DLC) / §11.7 | Packages on the Xbox CDN, dated by package modification date: **DLC Weapon 5** (2013-05-07, Assault SMG), **DLC Weapon 7** (2013-05-07, Bullpup Shotgun), **DLC Weapon 1** (2013-05-20, Heavy Rifle), **DLC Weapon 2** (2013-05-20, "Programmable Assault Rifle"), **DLC Weapon 3** (2013-05-20, Assault MG), **DLC Weapon 6** (2013-07-04, Rubber Gun), **Assault Sniper DLC** (2013-07-04), **Atomic Blimp DLC** (2013-07-04; Hidden Palace says the retail package was modified 2013-07-27). | ⚠️ | [5] |
| GTA V PS3 **Patch Data prototypes** (dev-kit HDD recoveries, BLES01807) | §8.5 (PS3 TU 1.01/1.02) | **Build 377** (date 2013-09-13), a 1.01 prototype that sits between retail base build 369 and retail 1.01 build 388.2; dumped by MrPinball64 in 2023. **Build 388.5** (date 2013-10-03), corresponding to TU **1.02**; released 2024-04-14 and extracted 2025-02-12. Hidden Palace notes that the retail 1.02 package itself is lost. | ⚠️ | [6] |
| GTA V PC **"GTA5 Debug.exe" + .pdb** | §8.6 / §11.7 | Executable date 2020-09-30 (Hidden Palace "Leftover Sep 30, 2020 Data"). | ⚠️ | [7] |

Cloud cache files were extracted on 2025-07-14. These are assets, not runnable builds. | ⚠️ | [7] |

Platforms named by Rockstar: **iOS (App Store), Android (Google Play), Windows Phone, PlayStation Vita**. GTA Wiki adds Amazon Appstore and Windows 8/8.1/RT/10 (Microsoft Store product 9WZDNCRFJ1ZP).
